# Automated CyberArk & Active Directory Lab Provisioning

This repository contains a fully automated, Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) deployment pipeline for provisioning a clustered Active Directory environment and pre-staging a CyberArk Privileged Access Management (PAM) architecture on Proxmox VE.

## 🏗️ Architecture Overview

The automation handles end-to-end deployment, moving from bare-metal VM clones to a fully functional, highly available corporate domain in minutes.

* **Hypervisor:** Proxmox VE cluster (utilizing local LVM storage and Cloudbase-Init).
* **Domain:** `lab.cybermark.tech`
* **Provisioning Method:** Zero-touch Windows Sysprep utilizing `SetupComplete.cmd` for background WinRM activation.
* **Core Infrastructure:**
  * Primary and Secondary Windows Server Domain Controllers (`dc-01`, `dc-02`).
  * Clustered DHCP Failover (Hot Standby).
  * Enterprise Root Certificate Authority (AD CS) for PKI.
* **CyberArk Pre-Staging:**
  * Dedicated Organizational Units (Tier 0, Tier 1, Infrastructure, CyberArk_Roles).
  * Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Security Groups.
  * Component VMs pre-allocated via DHCP reservations (Vault, PVWA, CPM, PSM, PSMP, HTMLGW).

---

## 🖥️ Phase 1: Building the Ansible Control Node

The automation is designed to run from a dedicated Linux control node. The following steps outline how to configure a **Fedora/RHEL** environment to orchestrate the Proxmox and Windows endpoints without triggering Python virtual environment (venv) isolation errors.

### 1. Install pipx and Ansible
Ensure your system is up to date and install `pipx` to manage Ansible's isolated environments.

```bash
sudo dnf update -y
sudo dnf install pipx -y
pipx ensurepath
```
*(Note: Restart your terminal session after running ensurepath before proceeding.)*

Install the core Ansible package:

```bash
pipx install --include-deps ansible
```

### 2. Inject Required Dependencies
Ansible needs specific Python libraries to communicate with the Proxmox API and authenticate to Windows over WinRM. Inject these directly into Ansible's `pipx` environment:

```bash
pipx inject ansible proxmoxer pywinrm requests requests-ntlm
```

### 3. Install Ansible Galaxy Collections
Download the required community collections for Proxmox and Windows management:

```bash
ansible-galaxy collection install -r requirements.yml
```

---

## 📀 Phase 2: The Windows Server Golden Image Build

To achieve zero-touch deployment, the Proxmox master template must be built precisely. This ensures WinRM opens silently in the background and the Active Directory credentials do not race against Cloud-Init.

### 1. VM Hardware Configuration
Create a new VM in Proxmox with the following specifications:
* **Disks:** SCSI bus, `vm` (LVM) storage, Qcow2 format, Write-back cache. Check **Discard** and **SSD emulation**.
* **System:** Check **Qemu Agent**.
* **Network:** VirtIO (paravirtualized).
* **CD/DVD:** Attach both the Windows Server ISO and the latest stable **VirtIO Drivers ISO**.

### 2. OS & Driver Installation
1. Boot the VM and load the `vioscsi` driver during Windows setup to detect the hard drive.
2. Install Windows Server.
3. Open Device Manager and install the **NetKVM** and **Balloon** drivers from the VirtIO ISO.
4. Install the Proxmox Guest Agent (`qemu-ga-x86_64.msi`).

### 3. Background WinRM Initialization (`SetupComplete.cmd`)
To open firewall ports securely as `NT AUTHORITY\SYSTEM` before the lock screen loads:
1. Navigate to `C:\Windows\Setup\` and create a folder named `Scripts`.
2. Inside `Scripts`, create a text file named exactly `SetupComplete.cmd` and insert:

```cmd
@echo off
call winrm quickconfig -q
call winrm set winrm/config/winrs @{MaxMemoryPerShellMB="1024"}
call winrm set winrm/config @{MaxTimeoutms="1800000"}
call winrm set winrm/config/service/auth @{Basic="true"}
powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "Get-NetConnectionProfile | Set-NetConnectionProfile -NetworkCategory Private"
powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "Enable-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'Windows Remote Management'"
```

### 4. The Automation Engine (`unattend.xml`)
This file creates the dedicated `ansible` service account natively during Sysprep.
1. Open Notepad as **Administrator**.
2. Save the following code exactly to `C:\Windows\System32\Sysprep\unattend.xml`:

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<unattend xmlns="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:unattend">
    <settings pass="oobeSystem">
        <component name="Microsoft-Windows-International-Core" processorArchitecture="amd64" publicKeyToken="31bf3856ad364e35" language="neutral" versionScope="nonSxS" xmlns:wcm="[http://schemas.microsoft.com/WMIConfig/2002/State](http://schemas.microsoft.com/WMIConfig/2002/State)" xmlns:xsi="[http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance)">
            <InputLocale>en-US</InputLocale>
            <SystemLocale>en-US</SystemLocale>
            <UILanguage>en-US</UILanguage>
            <UILanguageFallback>en-US</UILanguageFallback>
            <UserLocale>en-US</UserLocale>
        </component>
        <component name="Microsoft-Windows-Shell-Setup" processorArchitecture="amd64" publicKeyToken="31bf3856ad364e35" language="neutral" versionScope="nonSxS" xmlns:wcm="[http://schemas.microsoft.com/WMIConfig/2002/State](http://schemas.microsoft.com/WMIConfig/2002/State)" xmlns:xsi="[http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance)">
            <OOBE>
                <HideEULAPage>true</HideEULAPage>
                <HideLocalAccountScreen>true</HideLocalAccountScreen>
                <HideOEMRegistrationScreen>true</HideOEMRegistrationScreen>
                <HideOnlineAccountScreens>true</HideOnlineAccountScreens>
                <HideWirelessSetupInOOBE>true</HideWirelessSetupInOOBE>
                <ProtectYourPC>3</ProtectYourPC>
            </OOBE>
            <UserAccounts>
                <LocalAccounts>
                    <LocalAccount wcm:action="add">
                        <Password>
                            <Value>REPLACE_WITH_ANSIBLE_PASSWORD</Value>
                            <PlainText>true</PlainText>
                        </Password>
                        <Description>Ansible Automation Account</Description>
                        <DisplayName>ansible</DisplayName>
                        <Group>Administrators</Group>
                        <Name>ansible</Name>
                    </LocalAccount>
                </LocalAccounts>
            </UserAccounts>
        </component>
    </settings>
</unattend>
```

### 5. Cloudbase-Init (The Network Bridge)
1. Install Cloudbase-Init for Windows. **UNCHECK** the options to run Sysprep and Reboot at the end of the installer.
2. Edit `C:\Program Files\Cloudbase Solutions\Cloudbase-Init\conf\cloudbase-init.conf`:

```ini
[DEFAULT]
username=Administrator
groups=Administrators
inject_user_password=true
first_logon_behaviour=no
netbios_host_name_compatibility=false
metadata_services=cloudbaseinit.metadata.services.configdrive.ConfigDriveService
plugins=cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.mtu.MTUPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.windows.createuser.CreateUserPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.windows.setuserpassword.SetUserPasswordPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.sethostname.SetHostNamePlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.windows.extendvolumes.ExtendVolumesPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.networkconfig.NetworkConfigPlugin
```

### 6. Sysprep & Seal
Open an Administrator command prompt, navigate to `C:\Windows\System32\Sysprep`, and execute:

```cmd
sysprep.exe /generalize /oobe /shutdown /unattend:unattend.xml
```
Once powered off, right-click the VM in the Proxmox UI and select **Convert to template**.

---

## 🚀 Phase 3: The Deployment Pipeline

### 1. Configure Secrets
Update the variables in `inventory/group_vars/all.yml`. You must provide your specific Proxmox API tokens and desired Active Directory domain passwords before execution.

### 2. Infrastructure Provisioning (Nuke & Pave)
To completely wipe existing lab VMs and clone fresh instances from the Proxmox template:

```bash
ansible-playbook -i inventory/hosts.yml playbooks/provision.yml --limit domain_controllers
```
> **Warning:** Running this playbook against existing hosts will force-stop the VMs, destroy their LVM disks, and deploy fresh clones. Ensure you use the `--limit` flag to protect non-target infrastructure.

### 3. Lab Configuration & AD Promotion
To configure the operating systems, build the Active Directory forest, force the Domain Admin password synchronization, set up DHCP/PKI, and pre-stage all CyberArk objects:

```bash
ansible-playbook -i inventory/hosts.yml playbooks/setup_lab.yml
```

### 4. Verification
The `setup_lab.yml` playbook automatically calls `validate_lab.yml` at the end of its run. A successful run will print a green `>>> LAB STATUS REPORT <<<` confirming the environment is production-ready.

---

## 🛠️ Phase 4: Troubleshooting & Maintenance

### LVM Template Disk Locks (Proxmox swtpm exit 256)
If you attempt to modify a template by removing `template: 1` from the Proxmox `.conf` file, the VM will fail to boot because the LVM volumes are locked as read-only (`base-XXXX-disk`). 
* **The Fix:** Always clone the template to a new VMID (Full Clone), make your modifications, reseal with Sysprep, and convert the new clone into your master template.

### Active Directory Race Conditions (Error 1326)
If Windows Sysprep overwrites the Cloud-Init Administrator password during domain promotion, Ansible will fail to authenticate to the new domain.
* **The Fix:** This is already mitigated in `playbooks/setup_lab.yml` via **PLAY 2.5**, which uses the dedicated local `ansible` WinRM account to explicitly push the vault password to the AD database (`net user Administrator "PASSWORD" /domain`) immediately after promotion.

========================================
---
- name: "Provision Lab Infrastructure"
  hosts: windows,linux
  gather_facts: no
  serial: 2           # Critical: Process one VM at a time to save Storage I/O
  vars_files:
    - ../inventory/group_vars/all.yml

  roles:
    - role: proxmox_vm


========================================
FILE: ./playbooks/20_deploy_cyberark.yml
========================================
---
- name: Map custom IP variable to ansible_host
  hosts: cyberark_components
  gather_facts: false
  tasks:
    - name: Set ansible_host for WinRM connections
      set_fact:
        ansible_host: "{{ ip }}"

- name: Deploy Password Vault Web Access (PVWA)
  hosts: pvwa
  gather_facts: yes
  roles:
    - role: pvwa
      vars:
        pvwa_extract: true
        pvwa_ignore_checksum: true
        pvwa_prerequisites: true
        pvwa_install: true
        pvwa_hardening: true
        pvwa_registration: true
        pvwa_clean: true

- name: Deploy Central Policy Manager (CPM)
  hosts: cpm
  gather_facts: yes
  serial: 1
  roles:
    - role: cpm
      vars:
        cpm_extract: true
        cpm_ignore_checksum: true
        cpm_prerequisites: true
        cpm_install: true
        cpm_hardening: true
        cpm_registration: true
        cpm_clean: true

- name: Deploy Privileged Session Manager (PSM)
  hosts: psm
  gather_facts: yes
  roles:
    - role: psm
      vars:
        psm_extract: true
        psm_ignore_checksum: true
        psm_prerequisites: true
        psm_install: true
        psm_postinstall: true
        psm_hardening: true
        psm_registration: true
        psm_clean: true


========================================
FILE: ./playbooks/99_restore_clean_state.yml
========================================
---
- name: "Restore VM to 'Fresh_Install' State"
  hosts: all
  gather_facts: no
  connection: local
  serial: 1  # Restore one at a time to prevent storage overload

  vars:
    snapshot_name: "Fresh_Install"

  tasks:
    # --- SAFETY CHECK ---
    - name: "SAFETY CHECK: Confirm Restore for {{ inventory_hostname }}"
      pause:
        prompt: "WARNING: You are about to wipe {{ inventory_hostname }} and revert to the 'Fresh_Install' state. Press Enter to continue or Ctrl+C to abort."
      when: ansible_play_batch | length > 1 # Only ask if running on multiple, or remove this condition to always ask

    # --- 1. STOP VM (Required for Rollback) ---
    - name: "1. Force Stop VM {{ vmid }}"
      community.proxmox.proxmox_kvm:
        api_host: "{{ proxmox_api_host }}"
        api_user: "{{ proxmox_user }}"
        api_token_id: "{{ proxmox_token_id }}"
        api_token_secret: "{{ proxmox_token_secret }}"
        node: "{{ target_node }}"
        vmid: "{{ vmid }}"
        state: stopped
        force: yes
        timeout: 60
      delegate_to: localhost

    # --- 2. ROLLBACK SNAPSHOT ---
    - name: "2. Rollback to Snapshot: {{ snapshot_name }}"
      community.proxmox.proxmox_snap:
        api_host: "{{ proxmox_api_host }}"
        api_user: "{{ proxmox_user }}"
        api_token_id: "{{ proxmox_token_id }}"
        api_token_secret: "{{ proxmox_token_secret }}"
        # --- FIX: Change 'node' to 'hostname' or remove it entirely ---
        # The error says 'node' is unsupported but 'hostname' is supported.
        hostname: "{{ target_node }}"
        vmid: "{{ vmid }}"
        snapname: "{{ snapshot_name }}"
        state: rollback
      delegate_to: localhost
      register: rollback_status

    # --- 3. START VM ---
    - name: "3. Start VM {{ vmid }}"
      community.proxmox.proxmox_kvm:
        api_host: "{{ proxmox_api_host }}"
        api_user: "{{ proxmox_user }}"
        api_token_id: "{{ proxmox_token_id }}"
        api_token_secret: "{{ proxmox_token_secret }}"
        node: "{{ target_node }}"
        vmid: "{{ vmid }}"
        state: started
      delegate_to: localhost

    # --- 4. WAIT FOR CONNECTIVITY ---
    - name: "4. Wait for WinRM Port (5985)"
      wait_for:
        host: "{{ ip }}"
        port: 5985
        state: started
        timeout: 300
        delay: 5
      delegate_to: localhost
      when: ostype == 'win10' or ostype == 'win2019'

    - name: "4. Wait for SSH Port (22)"
      wait_for:
        host: "{{ ip }}"
        port: 22
        state: started
        timeout: 300
        delay: 5
      delegate_to: localhost
      when: ostype == 'l26' or ostype == 'linux'

FILE: ./playbooks/files/cloudbase-init.conf
========================================
[DEFAULT]
username=Administrator
inject_user_password=true
first_logon_behavior=no
metadata_services=cloudbaseinit.metadata.services.nocloudservice.NoCloudConfigDriveService
plugins=cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.mtu.MTUPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.networkconfig.NetworkConfigPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.windows.extendvolumes.ExtendVolumesPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.sethostname.SetHostNamePlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.userdata.UserDataPlugin, cloudbaseinit.plugins.common.localscripts.LocalScriptsPlugin
allow_reboot=false

========================================
FILE: ./playbooks/11_configure_ad_pki.yml
========================================
---
# ==============================================================================
# PLAY 1: INFRASTRUCTURE CHECK
# ==============================================================================
- name: "Pre-Check: Ensure DCs are Online"
  hosts: localhost
  gather_facts: no
  vars:
    target_dcs:
      - dc-01
      - dc-02
  tasks:
    - name: "Start Domain Controllers"
      community.proxmox.proxmox_kvm:
        api_host: "{{ hostvars[item]['proxmox_api_host'] | default('proxmox01.int.cybermark.tech') }}"
        api_user: "{{ hostvars[item]['proxmox_user'] | default('root@pam') }}"
        api_token_id: "{{ hostvars[item]['proxmox_token_id'] }}"
        api_token_secret: "{{ hostvars[item]['proxmox_token_secret'] }}"
        node: "{{ hostvars[item]['target_node'] | default('pve1') }}"
        vmid: "{{ hostvars[item]['vmid'] }}"
        state: started
      loop: "{{ target_dcs }}"
      ignore_errors: yes

    - name: "Wait for WinRM Port"
      ansible.builtin.wait_for:
        host: "{{ hostvars[item]['ansible_host'] }}"
        port: 5985
        state: started
        timeout: 600
        delay: 10
      loop: "{{ target_dcs }}"

# ==============================================================================
# PLAY 2: DOMAIN CONTROLLER INSTALLATION (Primary & Secondary)
# ==============================================================================
- name: "Install & Configure Domain Controllers"
  hosts: dc-01, dc-02
  gather_facts: yes
  roles:
    # Your role 'domain_controller' handles the 'when: inventory_hostname ==' logic
    - role: domain_controller

# ==============================================================================
# PLAY 2.5: PASSWORD SYNCHRONIZATION (Race Condition Fix)
# ==============================================================================
#- name: "Force Sync Domain Admin Password"
#  hosts: dc-01
#  gather_facts: no
#  vars_files:
#    - ../inventory/group_vars/all.yml
#  tasks:
#    - name: "Reset Domain Administrator Password in AD Database"
#      ansible.windows.win_shell: 'net user Administrator "{{ vault_lab_password }}" /domain'
#      register: password_sync
#      changed_when: "'The command completed successfully' in password_sync.stdout"
#

# ==============================================================================
# PLAY 3: DHCP FAILOVER
# ==============================================================================
- name: "Configure DHCP Failover"
  hosts: dc-01
  gather_facts: yes
  vars:
    # Pass variables to the role
    dhcp_scope_id: "10.0.3.0"
    dhcp_secondary_dc: "dc-02"
  roles:
    - role: dhcp_failover

# ==============================================================================
# PLAY 4: PKI & CORPORATE STRUCTURE
# ==============================================================================
- name: "Deploy PKI and AD Structure"
  hosts: dc-01
  gather_facts: yes
  roles:
    - role: pki
    - role: ad_structure

# ==============================================================================
# PLAY 5: FINAL VALIDATION
# ==============================================================================
- import_playbook: 90_validate_lab.yml


========================================
FILE: ./playbooks/00_build_win2025_template.yml
========================================
---
# ==========================================================
# PHASE 1: Proxmox VM Creation via SSH (Runs on Localhost)
# ==========================================================
- name: "Build Windows Server 2025 Golden Images (SSH Edition)"
  hosts: localhost
  gather_facts: no
  vars_files:
    - inventory/group_vars/all.yml
  vars:
    target_templates:
      - node: "proxmox02.mgmt.cybermark.tech"
        vmid: 9002
        name: "win2025-template-02"
        ip: "10.0.3.200"
        iso_main: "local:iso/win2025_slipstreamed-9002.iso"
      - node: "proxmox03.mgmt.cybermark.tech"
        vmid: 9003
        name: "win2025-template-03"
        ip: "10.0.3.201"
        iso_main: "local:iso/win2025_slipstreamed-9003.iso"

    iso_virtio: "local:iso/virtio-win-0.1.271.iso"
    ssh_cmd: "ssh -i ~/.ssh/id_proxmox_build -o StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new"

  tasks:
    - name: "STEP 1A: Force stop existing VMs"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ item.node }} 'qm stop {{ item.vmid }}'"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"
      ignore_errors: yes

    - name: "STEP 1B: Remove existing VMs"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ item.node }} 'qm destroy {{ item.vmid }} --destroy-unreferenced-disks 1'"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"
      ignore_errors: yes

    - name: "STEP 2: Create VM Shell"
      command: >
        {{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ item.node }}
        "qm create {{ item.vmid }} --name {{ item.name }} --memory 4096 --cores 2 --cpu host --ostype win11 --scsihw virtio-scsi-pci --bios ovmf --machine q35 --agent 1"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"

    - name: "STEP 2.5: Configure Disks, CD-ROMs, and TPM"
      command: >
        {{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ item.node }}
        "qm set {{ item.vmid }}
        --scsi0 {{ storage_id | default('vm') }}:60,cache=writeback,discard=on,ssd=1
        --efidisk0 {{ storage_id | default('vm') }}:1,format=raw,efitype=4m,pre-enrolled-keys=1
        --tpmstate0 {{ storage_id | default('vm') }}:1,version=v2.0
        --sata0 {{ item.iso_main }},media=cdrom
        --sata1 {{ iso_virtio }},media=cdrom
        --net0 virtio,bridge={{ bridge | default('vmbr0') }},tag={{ vlan_id | default('30') }}
        --boot 'order=scsi0;sata0'"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"

    - name: "STEP 2.7: Boot the VM"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ item.node }} 'qm start {{ item.vmid }}'"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"

    - name: "STEP 2.8: Bypass 'Press any key' prompt"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ item.node }} 'sleep 8; qm sendkey {{ item.vmid }} ret'"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"

    - name: "Wait for Windows to Install and open WinRM (Takes 10-15 mins)"
      ansible.builtin.wait_for:
        host: "{{ item.ip }}"
        port: 5985
        delay: 120
        timeout: 1800
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"

    - name: "Dynamically add new VMs to in-memory inventory for WinRM"
      ansible.builtin.add_host:
        name: "{{ item.ip }}"
        groups: build_vms
        ansible_user: "Administrator"
        ansible_password: "BuildPass123!"
        ansible_connection: "winrm"
        ansible_winrm_server_cert_validation: "ignore"
        ansible_port: 5985
        ansible_winrm_transport: ntlm
        target_node: "{{ item.node }}"
        vmid: "{{ item.vmid }}"
      loop: "{{ target_templates }}"

# ==========================================================
# PHASE 2: Windows Configuration via WinRM (Runs in Parallel)
# ==========================================================
- name: "Configure OS and Sysprep"
  hosts: build_vms
  gather_facts: no
  tasks:
    - name: "STEP 5.5A: Force gpsvc to timeout after 30 seconds"
      ansible.windows.win_regedit:
        path: HKLM:\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Winlogon
        name: GpNetworkStartTimeoutPolicyValue
        data: 30
        type: dword

    - name: "STEP 5.5B: Disable synchronous Foreground Policy processing"
      ansible.windows.win_regedit:
        path: HKLM:\SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Windows\System
        name: SyncForegroundPolicy
        data: 0
        type: dword

    - name: "STEP 5.5C: Hardcode WinRM Firewall Rule (Prevents post-reboot lockout)"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: Enable-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'Windows Remote Management'

    - name: "STEP 6: Install Windows Updates"
      ansible.windows.win_updates:
        category_names: ['CriticalUpdates', 'SecurityUpdates']
        reboot: yes
        reboot_timeout: 3600

    - name: "STEP 7: Prepare SetupComplete & Sysprep XML"
      ansible.windows.win_file:
        path: C:\Windows\Setup\Scripts
        state: directory

    - name: "Drop SetupComplete.cmd"
      ansible.windows.win_copy:
        dest: C:\Windows\Setup\Scripts\SetupComplete.cmd
        content: |
          @echo off
          call winrm quickconfig -q
          call winrm set winrm/config/winrs @{MaxMemoryPerShellMB="1024"}
          call winrm set winrm/config @{MaxTimeoutms="1800000"}
          call winrm set winrm/config/service/auth @{Basic="true"}
          powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "Get-NetConnectionProfile | Set-NetConnectionProfile -NetworkCategory Private"
          powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "Enable-NetFirewallRule -DisplayGroup 'Windows Remote Management'"

    - name: "Drop Final unattend.xml for Sysprep"
      ansible.windows.win_copy:
        src: files/unattend.xml
        dest: C:\Windows\System32\Sysprep\unattend.xml

    - name: "Download Cloudbase-Init (Latest Stable)"
      ansible.windows.win_get_url:
        url: "https://www.cloudbase.it/downloads/CloudbaseInitSetup_Stable_x64.msi"
        dest: "C:\\CloudbaseInitSetup_Stable_x64.msi"

    - name: "Install Cloudbase-Init"
      ansible.windows.win_package:
        path: "C:\\CloudbaseInitSetup_Stable_x64.msi"
        arguments: '/qn /norestart'
        state: present

    - name: "Drop Cloudbase-Init Config"
      ansible.windows.win_copy:
        src: files/cloudbase-init.conf
        dest: "C:\\Program Files\\Cloudbase Solutions\\Cloudbase-Init\\conf\\cloudbase-init.conf"

    - name: "Arm Cloudbase-Init for the Clone Boot (The Correct Way)"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        # 1. Stop the service cleanly
        Stop-Service -Name cloudbase-init -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        
        # 2. Clear the LocalData state folder so it thinks it is a fresh deployment
        $localData = "C:\Program Files\Cloudbase Solutions\Cloudbase-Init\LocalData"
        if (Test-Path $localData) {
            Remove-Item "$localData\*" -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        }
        
        # 3. Clear any old logs from the template build
        $logFile = "C:\Program Files\Cloudbase Solutions\Cloudbase-Init\log\cloudbase-init.log"
        if (Test-Path $logFile) {
            Remove-Item $logFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        }
        
        # 4. Guarantee it starts on the next boot
        Set-Service -Name cloudbase-init -StartupType Automatic
        
        exit 0

    - name: "STEP 9: Sysprep & Power Off"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: "C:\\Windows\\System32\\Sysprep\\sysprep.exe /generalize /oobe /shutdown /unattend:C:\\Windows\\System32\\Sysprep\\unattend.xml"
      async: 600
      poll: 0

# ==========================================================
# PHASE 3: Convert to Template via SSH
# ==========================================================
- name: "Convert VMs to Templates"
  hosts: localhost
  gather_facts: no
  vars_files:
    - inventory/group_vars/all.yml
  vars:
    ssh_cmd: "ssh -i ~/.ssh/id_proxmox_build -o StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new"
  tasks:
    - name: "Wait for VMs to power off via SSH"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ hostvars[item]['target_node'] }} 'qm status {{ hostvars[item]['vmid'] }}'"
      register: vm_status_ssh
      until: "'status: stopped' in vm_status_ssh.stdout"
      retries: 40
      delay: 15
      loop: "{{ groups['build_vms'] }}"

    - name: "Remove CD-ROMs before templating"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ hostvars[item]['target_node'] }} 'qm set {{ hostvars[item]['vmid'] }} --delete sata0,sata1'"
      loop: "{{ groups['build_vms'] }}"

    - name: "STEP 10: Inject Cloud-Init Drive and Default Configurations"
      command: >
        {{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ hostvars[item]['target_node'] }} 
        "qm set {{ hostvars[item]['vmid'] }} 
        --ide2 {{ storage_id | default('vm') }}:cloudinit 
        --citype nocloud 
        --ciuser Administrator 
        --ipconfig0 ip=dhcp"
      loop: "{{ groups['build_vms'] }}"

    - name: "Convert VMs to Templates via SSH"
      command: "{{ ssh_cmd }} root@{{ hostvars[item]['target_node'] }} 'qm template {{ hostvars[item]['vmid'] }}'"
      loop: "{{ groups['build_vms'] }}"

========================================
FILE: ./playbooks/90_validate_lab.yml
========================================
---
- name: "Lab Validation & Health Check (Production Standard)"
  hosts: dc-01
  gather_facts: no
  vars_files:
    - ../inventory/group_vars/all.yml
  vars:
    ansible_connection: winrm
    ansible_user: "administrator@lab.cybermark.tech"
    ansible_password: "{{ vault_ansible_password }}"

    # Validation Targets
    target_user: "admin.vault"
    target_group: "CyberArk_Vault_Admins"
    target_ou: "OU=Tier0,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}"
    target_computer: "pvwa-01"
    
    # --- CRITICAL: MATCHES YOUR PKI ROLE CA NAME ---
    ca_common_name: "CyberArk-Lab-Root-CA" 

  tasks:
# --- 1. CORE AD SERVICES ---
    - name: "1. Check Active Directory Services"
      ansible.windows.win_service_info:
        name: "{{ item }}"
      register: svc_info
      loop:
        - adws       # Active Directory Web Services
        - ntds       # Active Directory Domain Services
        - dns        # DNS Server
        - dhcpserver # DHCP Server
        - certsvc    # Active Directory Certificate Services
        - w3svc      # IIS (Required for CRL)
      # FIX: Correctly reference the registered variable
      failed_when: not svc_info.exists or svc_info.services[0].state != 'started'

    # --- 2. PKI & CERTIFICATE SERVICES CHECK ---
    - name: "2a. Verify Enterprise CA is Installed & Trusted"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        $CAName = "{{ ca_common_name }}"
        
        # 1. Check Registry (Confirms this server IS the CA)
        $RegPath = "HKLM:\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Services\CertSvc\Configuration\$CAName"
        if (-not (Test-Path $RegPath)) { 
            throw "CA Configuration for '$CAName' not found in Registry. Is the CA name correct?" 
        }

        # 2. Check Trusted Root Store (Confirms the server TRUSTS itself)
        $RootCert = Get-ChildItem Cert:\LocalMachine\Root | Where-Object { $_.Subject -match $CAName }
        if (-not $RootCert) {
            throw "The CA Certificate is not in the Trusted Root Store."
        }
        
        Write-Output "✅ CA Found & Trusted: $($RootCert.Subject)"
      register: ca_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: ca_check.rc != 0
    - name: "2b. Verify CRL (Revocation List) Accessibility"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        # Check if CRL exists on the filesystem (IIS Root)
        $CrlPath = "C:\Windows\System32\CertSrv\CertEnroll\{{ ca_common_name }}.crl"
        if (-not (Test-Path $CrlPath)) { throw "CRL file not found at $CrlPath" }
        
        # Check HTTP Accessibility
        $crlUrl = "http://localhost/CertEnroll/{{ ca_common_name }}.crl"
        try {
            $request = [System.Net.WebRequest]::Create($crlUrl)
            $response = $request.GetResponse()
            $response.Close()
            Write-Output "CRL is reachable via HTTP"
        } catch {
            throw "Failed to reach CRL via HTTP at $crlUrl. IIS might be misconfigured."
        }
      register: crl_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: crl_check.rc != 0

    # --- 3. CORPORATE STRUCTURE CHECK ---
    - name: "3a. Verify OU Structure (Tier 0)"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        if (-not (Get-ADOrganizationalUnit -Identity "{{ target_ou }}" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
             throw "OU '{{ target_ou }}' missing. Did ad_structure role run?"
        }
      register: ou_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: ou_check.rc != 0

    - name: "3b. Verify Vault Admin '{{ target_user }}' Exists"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        if (-not (Get-ADUser -Identity "{{ target_user }}" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
             throw "User '{{ target_user }}' missing."
        }
      register: user_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: user_check.rc != 0

    - name: "3c. Verify Security Group '{{ target_group }}'"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        if (-not (Get-ADGroup -Identity "{{ target_group }}" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
             throw "Group '{{ target_group }}' missing."
        }
      register: group_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: group_check.rc != 0

    - name: "3d. Verify Computer Object '{{ target_computer }}'"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        if (-not (Get-ADComputer -Identity "{{ target_computer }}" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
             throw "Computer '{{ target_computer }}' missing."
        }
      register: computer_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: computer_check.rc != 0

    # --- 4. INFRASTRUCTURE HEALTH ---
    - name: "4a. Verify DNS Resolution (Internal)"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Resolve-DnsName -Name "dc-02.{{ domain_name }}" -Type A
      register: dns_check
      changed_when: false
      failed_when: dns_check.rc != 0

    - name: "4b. Verify DHCP Scope is Active"
      ansible.windows.win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        $Scope = Get-DhcpServerv4Scope | Where-Object { $_.State -eq 'Active' }
        if (-not $Scope) { throw "No Active DHCP Scopes Found" }
        Write-Output "Scope $($Scope.ScopeId) is Active"
      register: dhcp_check
      changed_when: false

    # --- REPORTING ---
    - name: ">>> LAB STATUS REPORT <<<"
      debug:
        msg:
          - "✅ AD Services:    RUNNING"
          - "✅ PKI Service:    RUNNING (CA: {{ ca_common_name }})"
          - "✅ CRL Access:     VALID (HTTP Reachable)"
          - "✅ OU Structure:   VALID (Tier 0 Found)"
          - "✅ User Check:     VALID ({{ target_user }})"
          - "✅ Computer Check: VALID ({{ target_computer }})"
          - "✅ DNS Health:     RESOLVED"
          - "✅ DHCP Status:    ACTIVE"

FILE: ./requirements.yml

---
collections:
  # Required for Proxmox VM cloning, power management, and API interaction
  - name: community.proxmox
    version: ">=1.5.0"

  # Required for native Windows configuration, AD roles, and WinRM tasks
  - name: ansible.windows
    version: ">=2.2.0"

  # Required for extended Windows features (like AD domain joining and DHCP modules)
  - name: community.windows
    version: ">=2.1.0"

roles:
  - name: pvwa
    src: https://github.com/cyberark/pvwa.git
    scm: git
  - name: cpm
    src: https://github.com/cyberark/cpm.git
    scm: git
  - name: psm
    src: https://github.com/cyberark/psm.git
    scm: git


========================================
FILE: ./roles/ad_structure/tasks/main.yml
========================================
---
# ==============================================================================
# CORPORATE DIRECTORY STRUCTURE (Production Standard)
# ==============================================================================

- name: "1. Create Organizational Units (Corporate Hierarchy)"
  microsoft.ad.ou:
    name: "{{ item.name }}"
    path: "{{ item.path }}"
    state: present
    protect_from_deletion: yes
  loop:
    # --- ROOT ORGANIZATION ---
    - { name: "Corp",           path: "{{ domain_dn }}" }

    # --- ADMINISTRATIVE TIER (Privileged Accounts) ---
    - { name: "Administration", path: "OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" }
    - { name: "Tier0",          path: "OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" } # Vault Admins / Domain Admins
    - { name: "Tier1",          path: "OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" } # Server Admins
    - { name: "CyberArk_Roles", path: "OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" } # RBAC Groups

    # --- INFRASTRUCTURE (Servers & Computers) ---
    - { name: "Infrastructure", path: "OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" }
    - { name: "Servers",        path: "OU=Infrastructure,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" }
    - { name: "Management",     path: "OU=Servers,OU=Infrastructure,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" } # PVWA/CPM
    - { name: "Proxies",        path: "OU=Servers,OU=Infrastructure,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" } # PSM/PSMP

    # --- EMPLOYEES (Standard Users) ---
    - { name: "People",         path: "OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" }
    - { name: "Employees",      path: "OU=People,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" }

    # --- SERVICE ACCOUNTS ---
    - { name: "ServiceAccounts",path: "OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}" }

- name: "2. Create CyberArk Role-Based Groups"
  microsoft.ad.group:
    name: "{{ item.name }}"
    path: "{{ item.path }}"
    description: "{{ item.desc }}"
    scope: global
    category: security
    state: present
  loop:
    # --- TIER 0 ROLES (Security) ---
    - { name: "CyberArk_Vault_Admins",  path: "OU=CyberArk_Roles,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}", desc: "Full Control of the CyberArk Vault" }
    - { name: "CyberArk_Auditors",      path: "OU=CyberArk_Roles,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}", desc: "Read-Only Compliance Access" }

    # --- TIER 1 ROLES (Server Management) ---
    - { name: "Safe_Windows_Admins",    path: "OU=CyberArk_Roles,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}", desc: "Safe Access for Windows Server Credentials" }
    - { name: "Safe_Linux_Admins",      path: "OU=CyberArk_Roles,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}", desc: "Safe Access for Linux Server Credentials" }

    # --- PLATFORM GROUPS (Functional) ---
    - { name: "Windows_Server_Admins",  path: "OU=Tier1,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",          desc: "AD Group for Local Admin Rights on Windows Servers" }
    - { name: "Linux_Server_Admins",    path: "OU=Tier1,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",          desc: "AD Group for Sudo Rights on Linux Servers" }

    # --- TIER 2 ROLES (Business Users) ---
    - { name: "Safe_Business_Users",    path: "OU=CyberArk_Roles,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}", desc: "Safe Access for Standard Business Apps" }

- name: "3. Create Users (Production Identities)"
  microsoft.ad.user:
    # --- IDENTITY ---
    name: "{{ item.first }} {{ item.last }}"
    display_name: "{{ item.first }} {{ item.last }}"
    sam_account_name: "{{ item.name }}"
    upn: "{{ item.name }}@{{ domain_name }}"

    # --- STANDARD FIELDS ---
    firstname: "{{ item.first }}"
    surname: "{{ item.last }}"
    description: "{{ item.desc }}"
    path: "{{ item.path }}"
    password: "{{ vault_lab_password }}"
    state: present
    enabled: yes
    password_expired: yes

    # --- GROUPS ---
    groups: "{{ {'add': item.groups} if item.groups is defined else omit }}"

    # --- ATTRIBUTES ---
    attributes:
      set:
        department: "{{ item.dept | default(omit) }}"
        title: "{{ item.title | default(omit) }}"

  loop:
    # ==========================================
    # TIER 0: SECURITY & VAULT ADMINS
    # ==========================================
    - { name: "admin.vault",  first: "Vault",    last: "Admin",    path: "OU=Tier0,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",    groups: ["CyberArk_Vault_Admins", "Domain Admins"], desc: "Primary Vault Administrator", dept: "Security", title: "Security Architect" }
    - { name: "admin.audit",  first: "Compliance",last: "Auditor", path: "OU=Tier0,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",    groups: ["CyberArk_Auditors"],                      desc: "Internal Auditor", dept: "Audit",    title: "Compliance Officer" }

    # ==========================================
    # TIER 1: INFRASTRUCTURE ADMINS
    # ==========================================
    - { name: "admin.win",    first: "Windows",  last: "Admin",    path: "OU=Tier1,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",    groups: ["Windows_Server_Admins", "Safe_Windows_Admins"], desc: "Lead Windows Engineer", dept: "Infrastructure", title: "SysAdmin" }
    - { name: "admin.linux",  first: "Linux",    last: "Admin",    path: "OU=Tier1,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",    groups: ["Linux_Server_Admins", "Safe_Linux_Admins"],     desc: "Lead Linux Engineer",   dept: "Infrastructure", title: "DevOps Engineer" }
    - { name: "ops.support",  first: "Ops",      last: "Support",  path: "OU=Tier1,OU=Administration,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",    groups: ["Safe_Windows_Admins"],                          desc: "L2 Operations Support", dept: "Operations",     title: "Ops Analyst" }

    # ==========================================
    # TIER 2: STANDARD BUSINESS EMPLOYEES
    # ==========================================
    - { name: "alice.user",   first: "Alice",    last: "User",     path: "OU=Employees,OU=People,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",         groups: ["Safe_Business_Users"],                    desc: "HR Manager",         dept: "HR",       title: "Manager" }
    - { name: "bob.user",     first: "Bob",      last: "User",     path: "OU=Employees,OU=People,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",         groups: ["Safe_Business_Users"],                    desc: "Finance Analyst",    dept: "Finance",  title: "Analyst" }
    - { name: "charlie.user", first: "Charlie",  last: "User",     path: "OU=Employees,OU=People,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",         groups: ["Safe_Business_Users"],                    desc: "Sales Representative",dept: "Sales",   title: "Rep" }

    # ==========================================
    # SERVICE ACCOUNTS
    # ==========================================
    - { name: "svc_nessus",   first: "Service",  last: "Nessus",   path: "OU=ServiceAccounts,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",                                                                 desc: "Vulnerability Scanner Agent", dept: "SecOps", title: "Service Account" }
    - { name: "svc_backup",   first: "Service",  last: "Backup",   path: "OU=ServiceAccounts,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}",             groups: ["Domain Admins"],                          desc: "Legacy Backup Agent (Privileged)", dept: "Infra", title: "Service Account" }

- name: "4. Create LDAP Bind Account"
  microsoft.ad.user:
    name: "svc_cyberark_bind"
    firstname: "CyberArk"
    surname: "Bind"
    sam_account_name: "svc_cyberark_bind"
    upn: "svc_cyberark_bind@{{ domain_name }}"
    description: "Read-Only account for CyberArk Directory Mapping"
    path: "OU=ServiceAccounts,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}"
    password: "{{ vault_lab_password }}"
    enabled: yes
    state: present

- name: "5. Pre-stage CyberArk Component Servers"
  microsoft.ad.computer:
    name: "{{ item.name }}"
    # --- FIX: ADDED 'OU=' BEFORE THE ITEM VARIABLE ---
    path: "OU={{ item.ou }},OU=Servers,OU=Infrastructure,OU=Corp,{{ domain_dn }}"
    enabled: yes
    state: present
    attributes:
      set:
        description: "{{ item.desc }}"
  loop:
    # --- MANAGEMENT (Secure Zone) ---
    - { name: "pvwa-01",   ou: "Management", desc: "Password Vault Web Access 01" }
    - { name: "pvwa-02",   ou: "Management", desc: "Password Vault Web Access 02" }
    - { name: "cpm-01",    ou: "Management", desc: "Central Policy Manager 01" }
    - { name: "cpm-02",    ou: "Management", desc: "Central Policy Manager 02" }

    # --- PROXIES (Jump Zone) ---
    - { name: "psm-01",    ou: "Proxies",    desc: "Privileged Session Manager 01" }
    - { name: "psm-02",    ou: "Proxies",    desc: "Privileged Session Manager 02" }
    - { name: "psmp-01",   ou: "Proxies",    desc: "PSM for SSH 01" }
    - { name: "psmp-02",   ou: "Proxies",    desc: "PSM for SSH 02" }
    - { name: "htmlgw-01", ou: "Proxies",    desc: "HTML5 Gateway 01" }
    - { name: "htmlgw-02", ou: "Proxies",    desc: "HTML5 Gateway 02" }

========================================
FILE: ./roles/cpm/README.md
========================================
# CPM Ansible Role
This Ansible Role will deploy and install CyberArk Central Policy Manager including the pre-requisites, application, hardening and connect to an existing Vault environment.


## Requirements
------------
- Windows 2016 installed on the remote host
- WinRM open on port 5986 (**not 5985**) on the remote host 
- Pywinrm is installed on the workstation running the playbook
- The workstation running the playbook must have network connectivity to the remote host
- The remote host must have Network connectivity to the CyberArk vault and the repository server
  - 443 port outbound
  - 1858 port outbound 
- Administrator access to the remote host 
- CPM CD image


## Role Variables
These are the variables used in this playbook:

### Flow Variables
Variable                         | Required     | Default                                   | Comments
:--------------------------------|:-------------|:------------------------------------------|:---------
cpm_prerequisites                | no           | false                                     | Install CPM pre requisites
cpm_install                      | no           | false                                     | Install CPM
cpm_hardening                    | no           | false                                     | Apply CPM hardening 
cpm_registration                 | no           | false                                     | Connect CPM to the Vault
cpm_clean                        | no           | false                                     | N/A

### Deployment Variables
Variable                         | Required     | Default                                              | Comments
:--------------------------------|:-------------|:-----------------------------------------------------|:---------
vault_ip                         | yes          | None                                                 | Vault IP to perform registration
vault_port                       | no           | **1858**                                             | Vault port
vault_username                   | no           | **administrator**                                    | Vault username to perform registration
vault_password                   | yes          | None                                                 | Vault password to perform registration
secure_vault_password            | no           | None                                                 | Secure Vault password to perform registration
dr_vault_ip                      | no           | None                                                 | Vault DR IP address to perform registration
accept_eula                      | yes          | **"No"**                                             | Accepting EULA condition 
cpm_zip_file_path                | yes          | None                                                 | CyberArk CPM installation Zip file package path
cpm_installation_drive           | no           | **C:**                                               | Destination installation drive
cpm_username                     | no           | **PasswordManager**                                  | Vault Component's username

## Dependencies
None

## Usage
The role consists of a number of different tasks which can be enabled or disabled for the particular
run.

`cpm_install`

This task will deploy the CPM to required folder and validate successful deployment.

`cpm_hardening`

This task will run the CPM hardening process.

`cpm_registration`

This task will perform registration with active Vault.

`cpm_validateparameters`

This task will validate which CPM steps have already occurred on the server to prevent repetition.

`cpm_clean`

This task will clean the configuration (inf) files from the installation, delete the
CPM installation logs from the Temp folder and delete the cred files.

## Example Playbook
Below is an example of how you can incorporate this role into an Ansible playbook
to call the CPM role with several parameters:

```
---
- include_role:
    name: cpm
  vars:
    - cpm_prerequisites: true
    - cpm_install: true
    - cpm_hardening: true
    - cpm_registration: true
```

## Running the  playbook:
For an example of how to incorporate this role into a complete playbook, please see the
**[pas-orchestrator](https://github.com/cyberark/pas-orchestrator)** example.

## License
Apache License, Version 2.0

FILE: ./roles/cpm/tasks/cpm_clean.yml
========================================
---
# tasks file for cpm_extract

- name: Find all inf files
  win_find:
    paths: "{{ cpm_installation_path }}\\Password Manager\\Logs"
    patterns: ['*.*']
  register: files_to_delete

- name: Delete all inf files
  win_file:
    path: "{{ item.path }}"
    state: absent
  with_items: "{{ files_to_delete.files }}"

- name: Clean Windows\\Temp folder
  win_find:
    paths: "C:\\Windows\\Temp"
    patterns: ['CPM*.*', 'InstallationAutomation*.*']
  register: files_to_delete

- name: Delete temp folder
  win_file:
    path: "{{ item.path }}"
    state: absent
  with_items: "{{ files_to_delete.files }}"

- name: Keep installation automation folder
  win_copy:
    src: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}"
    dest: "{{ cpm_base_bin_drive }}\\Cyberark\\CPM"
    remote_src: True
  ignore_errors: yes

- name: Delete extract folder
  win_file:
    path: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}"
    state: absent

- name: Clean run History
  win_shell: |
    try {
      $path = "HKCU:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Explorer\RunMRU"

      if (& { Test-Path $path } 2>&1) {
        $arr = (Get-Item -Path $path).Property
        foreach ($item in $arr)
        {
          if ($item -ne "(Default)")
          {
            Remove-ItemProperty -Path $path -Name $item -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
          }
        }
      }
    } catch {
      Write-Output "Error occured: $error"
      exit 1
    }
    exit 0

- name: Clean event logs
  win_shell: |
    try {
      wevtutil el | ForEach-Object { wevtutil cl "$_" }
    } catch {
      Write-Output "Error occured: $error"
      exit 1
    }

- name: Clean recycle bin
  win_shell: |
    try {
      $Recycler = (New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application).Namespace(0xa)
      $Recycler.items() | ForEach-Object { rm $_.path -Force -Recurse }
    } catch {
      Write-Output "Error occured: $error"
      exit 1
    }


========================================
FILE: ./roles/cpm/tasks/cpm_extract.yml
========================================
---
# tasks file for cpm_extract

- name: Create folder
  win_file:
    path: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}"
    state: directory

- name: Copy CPM package from local source
  win_copy:
    src: "{{ cpm_zip_file_path }}"
    dest: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}\\{{ cpm_artifact_name }}"
  register: st

- fail:
    msg: "{{ cpm_zip_file_path }} not exists, failing..."
  when: not st.checksum

- name: Check if package is valid using checksum
  fail:
    msg: "Checksum does not match, please validate your package."
  when:
    - st.checksum != cpm_sha1_checksum
    - not cpm_ignore_checksum

- name: Unzip the CPM package
  win_unzip:
    src: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}\\{{ cpm_artifact_name }}"
    dest: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}"
  when: cpm_official

- name: Create component folder
  win_file:
    path: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}\\{{ cpm_component_folder }}"
    state: directory
  when: not cpm_official

- name: Unzip the CPM package
  win_unzip:
    src: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}\\{{ cpm_artifact_name }}"
    dest: "{{ cpm_extract_folder }}\\{{ cpm_component_folder }}"
  when: not cpm_official

FILE: ./roles/cpm/tasks/cpm_hardening.yml
========================================
---
# tasks file for cpm_hardening

- name: Create user.ini file
  win_file:
    path: "{{ cpm_installation_path }}\\Password Manager\\Vault\\user.ini"
    state: touch

- name: Hardening Block
  block:

    - name: Set cpm_hardening_script in domain
      set_fact:
        cpm_hardening_script: "CPM_In_Domain_Hardening.ps1"
      when: ansible_windows_domain_member

    - name: Run CPM hardening
      win_shell: |
        $ErrorActionPreference = "SilentlyContinue"
        try {
          $Action = .\{{ cpm_hardening_script }}
        } catch {
          Write-Output $Error
        } finally {
          $Action | Out-File -FilePath "{{ cpm_hardening_log }}"
          $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_hardening_log }}" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
        }
        Write-Output $Result.isSucceeded
        if ($Result.isSucceeded -eq 2) {
          exit 1
        } else {
          exit 0
        }
      args:
        chdir: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}"

- name: Validate CPM hardening and stop service
  win_service:
    name: "{{ cpm_service_name }}"
    state: stopped
  register: cpm_service_info

- name: Validate CPM scanner hardening and stop service
  win_service:
    name: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_name }}"
    state: stopped
  register: cpm_scanner_service_info

- set_fact:
    cpm_hardened: true
  when: cpm_service_info.username != "LocalSystem" and cpm_scanner_service_info.username != "LocalSystem"


========================================
FILE: ./roles/cpm/tasks/cpm_install.yml
========================================
---
# tasks file for cpm installation

- name: Set installation folder on xml config file
  win_shell: |
    try
    {
        ### This script set the install directory received from var in the install config file
        $filePath = "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Installation\\InstallationConfig.xml"
        $xml = [xml](Get-Content $filePath)
        $step = $xml.SelectSingleNode("//Parameter[@Name = 'CPMInstallDirectory']")
        $step.Value = "{{ cpm_installation_path }}"
        $xml.Save($filePath)
        exit 0
    }
    catch
    {
        Write-Output "Error occured during SetAtrributeInXML"
        exit 1
    }

- name: Installation Block
  block:

    - name: Run CPM installation
      win_shell: |
        Set-Location "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\Installation"
        #$ErrorActionPreference = "SilentlyContinue"
        $Action = .\CPMInstallation.ps1
        $Action | Out-File -FilePath "{{ cpm_install_log }}"
        $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_install_log }}" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
        if ($Result.isSucceeded -ne 0) {
          exit 1
        } else {
          exit 0
        }
      args:
        chdir: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Installation"

    - name: Get log path for installation
      win_shell: |
        $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_install_log }}"
        Write-Output $Result
      register: log_result

    - name: Fetch installation log from path
      fetch:
        src: '{{ item.logPath }}'
        dest: '{{ lookup("config", "DEFAULT_LOG_PATH") | dirname }}/cpm/{{ inventory_hostname }}_install.log'
        flat: yes
      with_items:
        - "{{ log_result.stdout | from_json }}"

  rescue:

    - name: Rescue - Get log path for installation
      win_shell: |
        $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_install_log }}"
        Write-Output $Result
      register: log_result

    - name: Rescue - Fetch installation log from path
      fetch:
        src: '{{ item.logPath }}'
        dest: '{{ lookup("config", "DEFAULT_LOG_PATH") | dirname }}/cpm/{{ inventory_hostname }}_install.log'
        flat: yes
      with_items:
        - "{{ log_result.stdout | from_json }}"

    - name: Rescue - Fail installation stage
      fail:
        msg: 'ERROR: Installation failed. For more info check {{ lookup("config", "DEFAULT_LOG_PATH") | dirname }}/cpm/{{ inventory_hostname }}_install.log'

- name: Get log path for installation
  win_shell: |
    $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_install_log }}"
    Write-Output $Result
  register: log_result

- name: Validate CPM installation
  win_lineinfile:
    path: '{{ item.logPath }}'
    regexp: 'Operation Succeeded'
    state: present
    line: 'Operation Succeeded'
  with_items:
    - "{{ log_result.stdout | from_json }}"

- name: Check if CPM service created successfully
  win_service:
    name: "{{ cpm_service_name }}"
  register: cpm_service_info

- name: Check if CPM scanner created successfully
  win_service:
    name: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_name }}"
  register: cpm_scanner_service_info

- set_fact:
    cpm_exists: "{{ cpm_service_info.exists }}"
    cpm_scanner_exists: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_info.exists }}"

FILE: ./roles/cpm/tasks/cpm_registration.yml
========================================
---
# tasks file for cpm registration

- name: Registration Block
  block:
    - set_fact:
        vault_ip_str: "{{ vault_ip }}"

    - name: Concat DR vault ip to vault_ip_str
      set_fact:
        vault_ip_str: "{{ vault_ip_str }},{{ dr_vault_ip }}"
      when: dr_vault_ip is defined

    - name: Change Vault ip in CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml
      win_xml:
        path: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Registration\\CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml"
        fragment: "{{ vault_ip_str }}"
        xpath: "//Parameter[@Name = 'vaultip']"
        attribute: "Value"
        type: attribute

    - name: Change Vault Port in CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml
      win_xml:
        path: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Registration\\CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml"
        fragment: "{{ vault_port }}"
        xpath: "//Parameter[@Name = 'vaultPort']"
        attribute: "Value"
        type: attribute

    - name: Change Vault User in CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml
      win_xml:
        path: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Registration\\CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml"
        fragment: "{{ vault_username }}"
        xpath: "//Parameter[@Name = 'vaultUser']"
        attribute: "Value"
        type: attribute

    - name: Change CPM Install Directory in CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml
      win_xml:
        path: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Registration\\CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml"
        fragment: "{{ cpm_installation_path }}"
        xpath: "//Parameter[@Name = 'installDirectory']"
        attribute: "Value"
        type: attribute

    - name: Change CPM username in CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml
      win_xml:
        path: "{{ cpm_installationautomation_folder }}\\Registration\\CPMRegisterComponentConfig.xml"
        fragment: "{{ cpm_username }}"
        xpath: "//Parameter[@Name = 'username']"
        attribute: "Value"
        type: attribute
      when: cpm_username is defined

    - name: Run CPM registration
      win_shell: |
        Set-Location "{{ cpm_registrationtool_folder }}"
        #$ErrorActionPreference = "SilentlyContinue"
        $secStrObj = ConvertTo-SecureString -String "{{ secure_vault_password }}" -Force
        $Action = .\CPMRegisterCommponent.ps1 -spwdObj $secStrObj
        $Action | Out-File -FilePath "{{ cpm_registration_log }}"
        $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_registration_log }}" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
        if ($Result.isSucceeded -ne 0) {
          exit 1
        } else {
          exit 0
        }
      no_log: true

    - name: Get log path for registration
      win_shell: |
        $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_registration_log }}"
        Write-Output $Result
      register: log_result
      no_log: true

    - name: Fetch registration log from path
      fetch:
        src: '{{ item.logPath }}'
        dest: '{{ lookup("config", "DEFAULT_LOG_PATH") | dirname }}/cpm/{{ inventory_hostname }}_registration.log'
        flat: yes
      with_items:
        - "{{ log_result.stdout | from_json }}"

    - name: Start CPM service & set to auto
      win_service:
        name: "{{ cpm_service_name }}"
        start_mode: auto
        state: started

    - name: Start CPM scanner service & set to auto
      win_service:
        name: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_name }}"
        start_mode: auto
        state: started

    - name: Check if CPM service is running
      win_service:
        name: "{{ cpm_service_name }}"
      register: cpm_service_info

    - name: Check if CPM scanner service is running
      win_service:
        name: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_name }}"
      register: cpm_scanner_service_info

    - set_fact:
        cpm_registered: true
      when: cpm_service_info.state == "running" and cpm_scanner_service_info.state == "running"

  rescue:

    - name: Rescue - Get log path for registration
      win_shell: |
        $Result = Get-Content "{{ cpm_registration_log }}"
        Write-Output $Result
      register: log_result
      no_log: true

    - name: Rescue - Fetch registration log from path
      fetch:
        src: '{{ item.logPath }}'
        dest: '{{ lookup("config", "DEFAULT_LOG_PATH") | dirname }}/cpm/{{ inventory_hostname }}_registration.log'
        flat: yes
      with_items:
        - "{{ log_result.stdout | from_json }}"

    - name: Rescue - Fail registration stage
      fail:
        msg: 'ERROR: Registration failed. For more info check {{ lookup("config", "DEFAULT_LOG_PATH") | dirname }}/cpm/{{ inventory_hostname }}_registration.log'


========================================
FILE: ./roles/cpm/tasks/cpm_validateparameters.yml
========================================
---
# tasks file for cpm_validateparameters
- name: Check EULA is accepted
  fail:
    msg: "You must accept EULA to start the playbook"
  when: (accept_eula|type_debug != 'bool' and accept_eula|lower != "yes") or (accept_eula|type_debug == 'bool' and not accept_eula)

- name: Set secure vault password
  block:
    - name: Set default value for vault_password
      set_fact:
        vault_password: ''
      when: vault_password is undefined

    - name: Set default value for secure_vault_password
      set_fact:
        secure_vault_password: ''
      when: secure_vault_password is undefined

    - name: Check if the user use 2 passwords
      fail:
        msg: "You must provide either vault_password or secure_vault_password parameter"
      when:
        - (vault_password != '')
        - (secure_vault_password != '')

    - name: Convert to securestring
      win_shell: |
        $secure_vault_password_object = ConvertTo-SecureString "{{ vault_password }}" -AsPlainText -Force
        $secure_vault_password_string = ConvertFrom-SecureString $secure_vault_password_object
        write-output $secure_vault_password_string
      register: secure_vault_password_string
      when: vault_password != ''

    - name: Set varible for secured vault password
      set_fact:
        secure_vault_password: "{{ secure_vault_password_string.stdout_lines[0] }}"
        vault_password: ''
      when: secure_vault_password == ''
  when:
    - cpm_registration
  no_log: true

- name: Check if CPM was already installed
  win_service:
    name: "{{ cpm_service_name }}"
  register: cpm_service_info

- name: Check if CPM scanner was already installed
  win_service:
    name: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_name }}"
  register: cpm_scanner_service_info

- set_fact:
    cpm_exists: "{{ cpm_service_info.exists }}"
    cpm_scanner_exists: "{{ cpm_scanner_service_info.exists }}"

- name: Check if CPM is hardened
  set_fact:
    cpm_hardened: true
  when:
    - cpm_exists and cpm_scanner_exists
    - cpm_service_info.username != "LocalSystem" and cpm_scanner_service_info.username != "LocalSystem"

- name: Check if CPM is registered
  set_fact:
    cpm_registered: true
  when:
    - cpm_exists and cpm_scanner_exists
    - cpm_service_info.state == "running"

FILE: ./roles/dhcp_failover/tasks/main.yml

---
- name: "Configure Hot Standby Failover"
  ansible.windows.win_shell: |
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
    Import-Module DhcpServer -Force
    
    $Partner = "{{ dhcp_secondary_dc }}.{{ domain_name }}"
    $FailoverName = "{{ dhcp_failover_name | default('Lab-Failover') }}"
    $ScopeId = "{{ dhcp_scope_id }}"

    # 1. Authorize Partner (Silently ignore if already done)
    try { 
        Add-DhcpServerInDC -DnsName $Partner -IPAddress {{ hostvars[dhcp_secondary_dc]['ansible_host'] }} -ErrorAction Stop 
    } catch { 
        Write-Host "Partner Auth skipped or failed (non-critical)" 
    }

    # 2. Create Failover Relationship
    if (-not (Get-DhcpServerv4Failover -Name $FailoverName -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Write-Host "Creating Failover Relationship..."
        Add-DhcpServerv4Failover -Name $FailoverName -ScopeId $ScopeId -PartnerServer $Partner -Force
    }

    # 3. Enforce Hot Standby Mode
    Set-DhcpServerv4Failover -Name $FailoverName -Mode HotStandby -ReservePercent 5 -Force

  # --- CRITICAL FIX: DOUBLE HOP AUTHENTICATION ---
  become: yes
  become_method: runas
  become_user: "{{ domain_admin_user }}"
  vars:
    ansible_become_password: "{{ lab_password }}"

FILE: ./roles/domain_controller/tasks/main.yml
========================================
---
# ==========================================
# PRIMARY DC (DC-01) CONFIGURATION
# ==========================================
- name: "Install AD & DHCP Roles"
  ansible.windows.win_feature:
    name:
      - AD-Domain-Services
      - DHCP
      - RSAT-AD-PowerShell
    include_management_tools: yes
    state: present

- name: "Promote Primary DC"
  microsoft.ad.domain:
    dns_domain_name: "{{ domain_name }}"
    domain_netbios_name: "{{ domain_netbios }}"
    safe_mode_password: "{{ safe_mode_password }}"
    install_dns: yes
  register: ad_promo_primary
  when: inventory_hostname == 'dc-01'

- name: "Reboot Primary DC if Promoted"
  ansible.windows.win_reboot:
    msg: "Rebooting after AD Promotion"
    post_reboot_delay: 180
  when: 
    - inventory_hostname == 'dc-01'
    - ad_promo_primary.reboot_required

- name: "Create Reverse DNS Zone"
  ansible.windows.win_shell: |
    $ZoneName = "3.0.10.in-addr.arpa"
    if (-not (Get-DnsServerZone -Name $ZoneName -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Add-DnsServerPrimaryZone -NetworkId "10.0.3.0/24" -ReplicationScope Forest
    }
  register: dns_create
  retries: 20
  delay: 30
  until: dns_create is success
  when: inventory_hostname == 'dc-01'

- name: "Authorize DHCP & Create Scope"
  ansible.windows.win_shell: |
    Add-DhcpServerInDC -DnsName "{{ inventory_hostname }}.{{ domain_name }}" -IPAddress {{ ansible_host }}
    
    if (-not (Get-DhcpServerv4Scope -ScopeId 10.0.3.0 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Add-DhcpServerv4Scope -Name "LabScope" -StartRange 10.0.3.100 -EndRange 10.0.3.200 -SubnetMask 255.255.255.0 -State Active
        Set-DhcpServerv4OptionValue -ScopeId 10.0.3.0 -DnsDomain {{ domain_name }} -DnsServer {{ ansible_host }}
        Set-DhcpServerv4OptionValue -ScopeId 10.0.3.0 -Router {{ gateway }}
    }
  when: inventory_hostname == 'dc-01'

# --- THIS IS THE CRITICAL MISSING LOOP ---
- name: "Import Reservations from Inventory"
  ansible.windows.win_shell: |
    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
    $ScopeId = "10.0.3.0"
    $IP   = "{{ hostvars[item]['ip'] }}"
    $MAC  = "{{ hostvars[item]['mac'] }}".Replace(':', '-')
    $Name = "{{ item }}"
    $Zone = "{{ domain_name }}"
    $ReverseZone = "3.0.10.in-addr.arpa"

    # DHCP Reservation
    if (-not (Get-DhcpServerv4Reservation -IPAddress $IP -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Add-DhcpServerv4Reservation -ScopeId $ScopeId -IPAddress $IP -ClientId $MAC -Name $Name
    }

    # DNS A Record
    if (-not (Get-DnsServerResourceRecord -ZoneName $Zone -Name $Name -RRType A -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Add-DnsServerResourceRecordA -Name $Name -ZoneName $Zone -IPv4Address $IP
    }

    # DNS PTR Record
    $Octet = $IP.Split('.')[-1]
    if (-not (Get-DnsServerResourceRecord -ZoneName $ReverseZone -Name $Octet -RRType Ptr -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)) {
        Add-DnsServerResourceRecordPtr -Name $Octet -ZoneName $ReverseZone -PtrDomainName "$Name.$Zone"
    }
  # Combines both groups to get all IPs
  loop: "{{ groups['windows'] + groups['linux'] }}"
  when: 
    - inventory_hostname == 'dc-01'
    - hostvars[item]['ip'] is defined

# ==========================================
# SECONDARY DC (DC-02) CONFIGURATION
# ==========================================
- name: "Point DNS to Primary DC"
  ansible.windows.win_dns_client:
    adapter_names: '*'
    dns_servers:
      - "10.0.3.1"
  when: inventory_hostname == 'dc-02'

- name: "Promote Secondary DC"
  microsoft.ad.domain_controller:
    dns_domain_name: "{{ domain_name }}"
    domain_admin_user: "{{ domain_admin_user }}"
    domain_admin_password: "{{ domain_admin_password }}"
    safe_mode_password: "{{ safe_mode_password }}"
    install_dns: yes
    state: domain_controller
  register: ad_promo_secondary
  when: inventory_hostname == 'dc-02'

- name: "Reboot Secondary DC if Promoted"
  ansible.windows.win_reboot:
    msg: "Rebooting Secondary DC"
    post_reboot_delay: 180
  when: 
    - inventory_hostname == 'dc-02'
    - ad_promo_secondary.reboot_required

- name: "DHCP Post-Install (Secondary)"
  ansible.windows.win_shell: |
    Add-DhcpServerSecurityGroup -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Restart-Service dhcpserver
  when: inventory_hostname == 'dc-02'

- name: "Install AD Membership Packages"
  ansible.builtin.package:
    name:

      - samba-common
      - samba-common-tools
    state: present
